- **tick-create-after-success.doctest**: A scheduled script can declare `create-after-success: [{ path, args }]` — after a successful run, each entry is created from its card type's default template. That `path` is card-authored data (an agent writes scheduled-script cards), and this is a *write* path, so it goes through the shared ref algebra as a `write-target`: leading `/` means the box root, and a `..` that climbs out of the box reso ([ianb/beebox](https://github.com/ianb/beebox/blob/HEAD/beebox/test/cli/commands/tick-create-after-success.doctest.md) · ★1)
- **tick-engine-wait.doctest**: When the box's engine is out of quota (deferred-recoverable), the scheduler must not burn attempts: due scripts skip with a visible reason until the reset, and a run that failed *because* of the episode records a `deferred` outcome that freezes — neither increments nor resets — `consecutiveFailures`. ([ianb/beebox](https://github.com/ianb/beebox/blob/HEAD/beebox/test/cli/commands/tick-engine-wait.doctest.md) · ★1)
- **tick-force.doctest**: `bbx tick --script &lt;name&gt; --force` re-runs a script on demand: the schedule (due-ness), budget, and active-chat gates are bypassed (a forced run often originates from chat, so deferring on the chat would be self-defeating; the housekeeping-commit hazard is re-checked at the commit site instead). Gates that protect against real breakage stay: `enabled: false`, missing connectors, a live lock-group ([ianb/beebox](https://github.com/ianb/beebox/blob/HEAD/beebox/test/cli/commands/tick-force.doctest.md) · ★1)

- **to-tickets**: Slice an approved spec into thin vertical tickets and maintain them as a DAG. A vertical slice crosses every layer its feature touches, so integration is proven at the first commit instead of the last; horizontal slicing (all the schema, then all the endpoints) hides failure until everything is written. Each ticket declares what blocks it, which is what lets /implement pick safely and run independent slices in parallel on separate branches. Entry point for the ticket-board skill. Modes: slice a spec, board, next, show, add, block, status, reconcile. Backend and path come from ~/.claude/assay.… ([ShadyBad/assay](https://github.com/ShadyBad/assay/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/to-tickets.md) · ★1)

- **tommy**: Ponto de entrada único do Tommy. Recebe o pedido em linguagem natural e orquestra tudo na conversa principal: elicitação e spec (agente tommy-spec, perguntas repassadas ao usuário), revisão independente (tommy-product-review), aprovação da spec pelo usuário e, a partir daí, de forma autônoma: arquitetura (tommy-architect), planos (tommy-planner), análise dos planos (tommy-product-review) e código (tommy-codegen). Bugs seguem o fluxo de bugfix. ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy.md) · ★1)
- **tommy-bugfix**: Modo manual (/tommy também detecta bugs e segue este fluxo). Corrige um bug com rastreabilidade: avalia (reprodução + causa raiz, confirmada com o usuário), corrige via agente tommy-codegen (teste de regressão primeiro, correção mínima, quality gate) e verifica (veredito verified/partial/failed). ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy-bugfix.md) · ★1)
- **tommy-commit**: Instrui o agente Tommy Git a analisar as mudanças locais e criar commit(s) seguindo Conventional Commits. ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy-commit.md) · ★1)
- **tommy-open-pr**: Instrui o agente Tommy Git a detectar o provedor Git do projeto, subir a branch e abrir um Pull/Merge Request. ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy-open-pr.md) · ★1)
- **tommy-prompt**: Modo manual (o caminho padrão é /tommy, que roda esta etapa automaticamente). Cria um plano de implementação detalhado a partir de uma spec aprovada. Orquestra na conversa principal: gate de precondição, arquitetura (agente tommy-architect), preenchimento do plano com rastreabilidade por requisito e análise de consistência independente (agente tommy-product-review, modo plan-analysis). Nunca gera código de implementação — apenas planejamento. ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy-prompt.md) · ★1)
- **tommy-run-codegen**: Modo manual (o caminho padrão é /tommy, que roda o codegen automaticamente após a aprovação da spec). Instrui o agente Tommy Codegen a seguir um plano de implementação e gerar o código correspondente — o agente cuida de tudo: gates de precondição, implementação, quality gate, matriz de aceite e convergência spec→código. ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy-run-codegen.md) · ★1)
- **tommy-specify**: Modo manual (o caminho padrão é /tommy, que orquestra spec, planos e código). Cria ou atualiza a especificação de uma feature a partir de uma descrição em linguagem natural (ou refina uma spec existente). Orquestra na conversa principal: branch, elicitação de requisitos (skill business-analyst, método de árvore de decisão com resposta recomendada), escrita da spec com log de decisões e revisão independente (agente tommy-product-review, que também gera os checklists de domínio). ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy-specify.md) · ★1)
- **tommy-start**: Cria a base de conhecimento inicial do projeto para o Tommy. Verifica os pré-requisitos da máquina, gera o grafo de código (Graphify), lê o skill tommy-project-research e preenche .tommy/ (scaffolding, project-context com os princípios MUST/SHOULD, codebase, TOMMY.md) e o AGENTS.md na raiz. Ao final, propõe capacidades opt-in por stack: MCPs, permissões, CLAUDE.md, Sonar e find-skills. ([rwmsousa/sdd-tommy](https://github.com/rwmsousa/sdd-tommy/blob/HEAD/claude-code/commands/tommy-start.md) · ★1)

([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-retro.md) · ★1)
- **topia-review**: Code quality review — patterns, security, performance, correctness. Finds bugs, suggests improvements, triggers fix for issues found. Escalates to opus for security-critical code. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-review.md) · ★1)
- **topia-review-intake**: Use when receiving external input that needs structured intake before action — PR review feedback, code review comments, OR issue tracker items (bug reports, feature requests). PR Review Mode (default) verifies suggestions before implementing. Issue Triage Mode classifies issues into a state machine (ready-for-agent / ready-for-human / needs-info / wontfix) and emits AGENT-BRIEFs for AFK execution. Prevents blind implementation, enforces verification-first discipline. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-review-intake.md) · ★1)
- **topia-safeguard**: Build safety nets before refactoring. Use when running surgeon or any risky refactor that needs a rollback point. Creates characterization tests, boundary markers, config freezes, and rollback points. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-safeguard.md) · ★1)
- **topia-sast**: Static analysis tool runner. Wraps ESLint, Semgrep, Bandit, Clippy, and language-specific analyzers with unified severity output. Use when deeper code analysis needed beyond pattern matching. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-sast.md) · ★1)
- **topia-scaffold**: Autonomous project bootstrapper. Generates complete project from a description — structure, code, tests, docs, config. Orchestrates idea → plan → design → fix → test → docs → git in one pipeline. The \"0 to production-ready\" skill. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-scaffold.md) · ★1)
- **topia-sequential-thinking**: Step-by-step complex reasoning for multi-variable problems. Breaks interconnected decisions into ordered logical steps with bias detection, reversibility classification, and second-order effect tracking. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-sequential-thinking.md) · ★1)
- **topia-session-bridge**: Universal context persistence across sessions. Auto-saves decisions, conventions, and progress to .topia/ files. Loads state at session start. Use when any skill makes architectural decisions or establishes patterns that must survive session boundaries. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-session-bridge.md) · ★1)
- **topia-skill-forge**: Use when creating new Topia skills, editing existing skills, or verifying skill quality before deployment. Applies TDD discipline to skill authoring — test before write, verify before ship. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-skill-forge.md) · ★1)
- **topia-slides**: Generate Marp-compatible slide decks from structured JSON schema. Converts context into presentations for tech talks, sprint demos, and tutorials. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-slides.md) · ★1)
- **topia-surgeon**: Incremental refactorer. Use within a rescue workflow after safeguard has set up safety nets. Refactors ONE module per session using proven patterns — Strangler Fig, Branch by Abstraction, Expand-Migrate-Contract. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-surgeon.md) · ★1)
- **topia-team**: Multi-agent meta-orchestrator. Use when task spans 5+ files or 3+ modules, or when user says 'parallel', 'split this up', 'do all of these'. Decomposes large tasks into parallel workstreams, assigns to isolated build instances, coordinates merging. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-team.md) · ★1)
- **topia-test**: TDD test writer. Writes failing tests FIRST (red), then verifies they pass after implementation (green). Covers unit, integration, and e2e tests. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-test.md) · ★1)
- **topia-trend-scout**: Scan market trends, competitor activity, and emerging patterns. Monitors Product Hunt, GitHub Trending, HackerNews, and social platforms. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/commands/topia-trend-scout.md) · ★1)
- **topia-tut**: Re-shows the structured first-run menu (finalize / onboard / doctor / faq / help) on demand, with current completion status.
